---
title: "Gitea webhooks"
sidebarTitle: "Webhooks"
description: "Gitea incoming webhooks in Corsair: the event paths to subscribe to, the payload each event carries, and the response data."
---

The Gitea plugin handles incoming webhooks. Point your provider’s subscription URL at your Corsair HTTP handler (see [Overview](/plugins/gitea/overview) for setup context and the exact URL shape).

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [webhooks](/concepts/webhooks) and [hooks](/concepts/hooks).
</Info>

## Webhook map

- `fork` (`fork`)
- `issueComment` (`issueComment`)
- `issues` (`issues`)
- `package` (`package`)
- `pullRequest` (`pullRequest`)
- `pullRequestReview` (`pullRequestReview`)
- `push` (`push`)
- `refCreated` (`refCreated`)
- `refDeleted` (`refDeleted`)
- `release` (`release`)
- `repository` (`repository`)
- `status` (`status`)
- `wiki` (`wiki`)
- `workflowJob` (`workflowJob`)
- `workflowRun` (`workflowRun`)

## HTTP handler setup

```ts app/api/webhook/route.ts
import { processWebhook } from "corsair";
import { corsair } from "@/server/corsair";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
    const headers = Object.fromEntries(request.headers);
    // With multiTenancy, give each tenant its own URL (/api/webhook?tenantId=acme):
    // the query says whose account, secret and hooks handle the delivery.
    const query = Object.fromEntries(new URL(request.url).searchParams);
    // The raw text, not request.json(): signatures are checked over the exact bytes sent.
    const body = await request.text();
    const result = await processWebhook(corsair, headers, body, query);
    if (!result.plugin) {
        return Response.json({ success: false }, { status: 404 });
    }
    // A refusal (bad signature) carries no status and answers 400; a handler
    // that threw comes back with 500, so the sender retries it.
    const status =
        result.response?.success === false ? (result.response.statusCode ?? 400) : 200;
    return Response.json(result.response ?? { success: true }, {
        status,
        headers: result.responseHeaders,
    });
}
```

## Events

## Fork

### Fork

`fork`

The repository was forked (X-Gitea-Event-Type fork)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `forkee` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        fork: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Issue Comment

### Issue Comment

`issueComment`

A comment on an issue or pull request was created, edited or deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type issue_comment, pull_request_comment)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `issue` | `object \| null` | No | Issue represents an issue in a repository |
| `pull_request` | `object \| null` | No | PullRequest represents a pull request |
| `comment` | `object \| null` | No | Comment represents a comment on a commit or issue |
| `changes` | `object \| null` | No | What an edit changed: the old title, body or ref. |
| `is_pull` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        issueComment: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Issues

### Issues

`issues`

An issue was opened, edited, closed, reopened, assigned, labeled or given a milestone (X-Gitea-Event-Type issues, issue_assign, issue_label, issue_milestone)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `changes` | `object \| null` | No | What an edit changed: the old title, body or ref. |
| `issue` | `object \| null` | No | Issue represents an issue in a repository |
| `commit_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        issues: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Package

### Package

`package`

A package was published or deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type package)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `package` | `object \| null` | No | Package represents a package |
| `organization` | `object \| null` | No | Organization represents an organization |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        package: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Pull Request

### Pull Request

`pullRequest`

A pull request was opened, edited, closed, merged, synchronized, assigned, labeled, given a milestone or a review request (X-Gitea-Event-Type pull_request, pull_request_assign, pull_request_label, pull_request_milestone, pull_request_sync, pull_request_review_request)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `changes` | `object \| null` | No | What an edit changed: the old title, body or ref. |
| `pull_request` | `object \| null` | No | PullRequest represents a pull request |
| `requested_reviewer` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |
| `commit_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `review` | `object \| null` | No | The review, on review events. |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="changes full type">

```ts
{
  title?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null,
  body?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null,
  ref?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```

</AccordionGroup>

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        pullRequest: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Pull Request Review

### Pull Request Review

`pullRequestReview`

A pull request review was submitted: approved, changes requested, or a review comment (X-Gitea-Event-Type pull_request_review_approved, pull_request_review_rejected, pull_request_review_comment)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `changes` | `object \| null` | No | What an edit changed: the old title, body or ref. |
| `pull_request` | `object \| null` | No | PullRequest represents a pull request |
| `requested_reviewer` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |
| `commit_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `review` | `object \| null` | No | The review: its type and text. |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="changes full type">

```ts
{
  title?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null,
  body?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null,
  ref?: {
    from?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        pullRequestReview: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Push

### Push

`push`

Commits pushed to a branch or tag (X-Gitea-Event-Type push)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `ref` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `before` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `after` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `compare_url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `commits` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `total_commits` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `head_commit` | `object \| null` | No | PayloadCommit represents a commit |
| `pusher` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        push: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

## Ref Created

### Ref Created

`refCreated`

A branch or tag was created (X-Gitea-Event-Type create)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sha` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `ref` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `ref_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        refCreated: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

## Ref Deleted

### Ref Deleted

`refDeleted`

A branch or tag was deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type delete)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `ref` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `ref_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `pusher_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        refDeleted: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Release

### Release

`release`

A release was published, updated or deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type release)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `release` | `object \| null` | No | Release represents a repository release |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        release: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Repository

### Repository

`repository`

The repository was created or deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type repository)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `organization` | `object \| null` | No | Organization represents an organization |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        repository: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Status

### Status

`status`

A commit status was set (X-Gitea-Event-Type status)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `commit` | `object \| null` | No | PayloadCommit represents a commit |
| `context` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `sha` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `target_url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        status: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Wiki

### Wiki

`wiki`

A wiki page was created, edited or deleted (X-Gitea-Event-Type wiki)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `page` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `comment` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        wiki: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Workflow Job

### Workflow Job

`workflowJob`

A Gitea Actions job was queued, started or completed (X-Gitea-Event-Type workflow_job)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workflow_job` | `object \| null` | No | ActionWorkflowJob represents a WorkflowJob |
| `pull_request` | `object \| null` | No | PullRequest represents a pull request |
| `organization` | `object \| null` | No | Organization represents an organization |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="workflow_job full type">

```ts
{
  completed_at?: string | null,
```

**`webhookHooks` example**

```ts
gitea({
    webhookHooks: {
        workflowJob: {
            before(ctx, args) {
                return { ctx, args };
            },
            after(ctx, response) {
            },
        },
    },
})
```

---

## Workflow Run

### Workflow Run

`workflowRun`

A Gitea Actions workflow run was requested, started or completed (X-Gitea-Event-Type workflow_run)

**Payload**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workflow` | `object \| null` | No | ActionWorkflow represents a ActionWorkflow |
| `workflow_run` | `object \| null` | No | ActionWorkflowRun represents a WorkflowRun |
| `pull_request` | `object \| null` | No | PullRequest represents a pull request |
| `organization` | `object \| null` | No | Organization represents an organization |
| `repository` | `object \| null` | No | Repository represents a repository |
| `sender` | `object \| null` | No | User represents a user |
